- **Forced-re-render fix and the SPA-navigation detection retry** (2026-07-17) — both confirmed live: mid-episode provider switching applies immediately regardless of play/pause state, and episode-to-episode navigation needs no reload. See Decisions Log.
- **Amazon/Netflix (unbracketed direct-source) files not persisting a saved uploader preference across episodes** — confirmed live 2026-07-17 as expected behavior, not a bug (no bracket tag to extract, per `extractUploaderTag`'s existing design). See Decisions Log 2026-07-16 and 2026-07-17.
- **Auto-offset-detection on Jimaku import** — decided and scheduled into Phase 6, see Decisions Log 2026-07-05.
- **Numerals/numbers not clickable** — built, see Decisions Log 2026-07-05 and Section 5 (Grouping/merge pipeline).
- **Fansub-provider markup handling** — built, see Decisions Log 2026-07-05.
- **後藤-style multi-kanji name fusion** — built, see Decisions Log 2026-07-05 and Section 5 (Grouping/merge pipeline).
- **Colloquial-contraction handling (どした → どす)** — investigated, accepted as a permanent limitation, see Decisions Log 2026-07-05.
- **Inline furigana in subtitle files** (2026-07-01) — stripped via `INLINE_FURIGANA_RE` per user request (redundant with click-to-check, looked cluttered).
- **One Piece ep 894 forced-subtitle status** (2026-07-01) — confirmed this episode does NOT have Crunchyroll's forced-subtitle layer; that interaction remains untested (see Ongoing, "Forced-subtitle overlay behavior").
- **んだろう/んでしょう showing bare ん's wrong sense** (2026-07-01, second round) — turned out to be a missing-construction problem (JMdict has no headword for the extended forms), not an entry-ranking problem like こと/いる. Fixed with dedicated Rule 0.6, not entry ranking.
- **それも and ないと "no entry" assumptions** (2026-07-01, second round) — both turned out to have real JMdict entries the original bug report assumed didn't exist. それも: confirmed real, left as-is. ないと: also real, routed through the と dual-view carve-out specifically.
- **Anki export design** (2026-07-02) — AnkiConnect over offline export; core fields = word + reading + gloss + full sentence. See Decisions Log 2026-07-02.
- **Capture-action UX** (2026-07-02) — "Add to Anki" button on the existing popup, instant send, undo/edit-last-card after. See Decisions Log 2026-07-02.
- **こと-style JMdict sense-ordering** (2026-07-03) — solved via TUBELEX-frequency integration (`scripts/apply-tubelex-frequency.js`), a general re-ranking mechanism, not a こと-specific patch. See Decisions Log 2026-07-03.
- **でしょう/だろう resolving to です's own definition** (2026-07-03) — real bug (not the earlier, already-resolved んだろう construction issue), fixed in Rule 3's group-construction step. See Decisions Log 2026-07-03.
- **王都-style compound-noun ordering** (2026-07-03) — fixed by excluding pure noun+noun spans from dual-view classification. See Decisions Log 2026-07-03.
- **凱旋+です noun+copula mislabeling** (2026-07-03) — relabeled as "+ です (copula)" rather than suppressed. See Decisions Log 2026-07-03.
- **れる/られる, せる/させる, ば not merging into the preceding verb** (2026-07-03) — れる/られる/せる/させる was a real, never-working gap (confirmed not a regression); ば was genuinely new scope, approved via the litmus test. Both fixed. See Decisions Log 2026-07-03.
- **でしょう/だろう fix's bare-form gap** (2026-07-03) — original fix only covered でしょう/だろう with う present; extended to also cover colloquial bare だろ/でしょ. See Decisions Log 2026-07-03.
- **な (大事なことさ) showing だ+た as equally-weighted cards** (2026-07-03) — traced to the TUBELEX cross-reading contamination bug, fixed there; separately, な itself now gets its own inflection label ("な (attributive)") instead of showing none. See Section 5 (Homograph/sense ordering via TUBELEX; Popup content & display).
- **んだ/んです wrongly labeled "た-form"** (2026-07-03) — Rule 0.6's ん+copula-family construction was falling through to the generic verb-past-tense だ table entry; given its own "んだ (explanatory)" / "んです (explanatory, polite)" labels. See Section 5 (Popup content & display).
- **Inflection-label plain-English descriptors** (2026-07-03) — every label now carries a short parenthetical, with the copula labels split three ways by preceding word type. See Section 5 (Popup content & display).
- **Multi-reading entries displaying the wrong reading, prioritization** (2026-07-04) — decided to fix now rather than backlog it. Scheduled into Phase 4 Build Order. See Decisions Log 2026-07-04.
- **JLPT tag sourcing** (2026-07-04) — decided to source externally if jmdict-simplified lacks the data; toggle stays either way. See Decisions Log 2026-07-04.
- **Jimaku search-by-title mismatch** (2026-07-04) — decided in favor of TMDB/AniList ID resolution over a fuzzy-search fallback. Originally scheduled into Phase 6, moved to the new Phase 4.5 (2026-07-05) — see Decisions Log 2026-07-04 and 2026-07-05.
- **Jimaku per-season offset/uploader memory** (2026-07-04) — un-deferred; decided to build with a per-show/season caching design. Originally scheduled into Phase 6, moved to the new Phase 4.5 (2026-07-05) — see Decisions Log 2026-07-04 and 2026-07-05.
- **Romaji toggle investment level** (2026-07-04) — resolved by excluding it entirely, not a priority call. See Decisions Log 2026-07-04.
- **Shows with zero Jimaku subtitles available at all** (2026-07-04) — this is what the manual upload fallback handles; a zero-results state becomes an upload prompt, not a dead end. Built 2026-07-06. See Section 5 and Build Order (Phase 4).
- **Pricing specifics** (2026-07-04) — reviewed and reconfirmed: kept deferred to post-soft-launch, since it doesn't block any coding work. No change from the original 2026-06-30 pricing-model decision.
- **Offset-reset hotkey (⌥+0) not working** (2026-07-05) — Mac Alt-key character remapping; fixed via `event.code` instead of `event.key`. See Decisions Log 2026-07-05.
- **してい/いいん false phrase-matcher attachments** (2026-07-05) — traced to candidate spans cutting across an existing baseline group's boundary rather than a fuse-gate problem; fixed by generalizing the "already correctly grouped" filter to reject any boundary-misaligned span. See Section 5 (Phrase-matching) and Decisions Log.
- **な not detected as attributive after ひとりぼっち fuses, 時に false-fusing in 遠足の時に, だら/たら conditional not absorbing** (2026-07-05) — three real grouping gaps, all fixed. See Section 5 (Grouping/merge pipeline).
- **Contracted てる/てた/てく/てき, なく, and んだろう/んでしょう showing no or unhelpful inflection labels; うまくなる's うまく showing no label at all** (2026-07-05) — all fixed with new/extended `INFLECTION_LABELS` entries and `describeInflection()` branches. See Section 5 (Popup content & display).
- **Half-width katakana resolving correctly but still displaying half-width on screen** (2026-07-05) — the 2026-07-01 fix only normalized at the lookup layer; content.js now also normalizes the raw subtitle text before tokenization. See Section 5.
- **子's TUBELEX-scoring cross-contamination and ordering tie (ね vs こ)** (2026-07-05) — fixed by a POS-compatibility gate in `apply-tubelex-frequency.js`; the residual tie cleared by a full `jmdict-compact.json` regeneration. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **`jmdict-compact.json` regenerated from scratch** (2026-07-05) — the original 2026-06-24 generation script never survived in this repo; reconstructed and validated statistically (99.96%+ match) before trusting it. Extended with `rs`/`m`/`ki` fields for the multi-reading and duplicate-gloss fixes below. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **Kanji-containing proper nouns silently closing the popup on a lookup miss** (2026-07-05) — implements the 2026-07-04 decision; the special-case silent-close only ever affected kanji names in practice (katakana-only names are separately inert upstream), so it was removed outright. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **Multi-reading entries displaying the wrong reading (くる resolving to 刳る showing えぐる)** (2026-07-05) — fixed via the new `rs` field (full reading list per entry) plus a `lookupWord` check that swaps in whichever reading was actually used to reach the entry. See Section 5.
- **Duplicate-gloss homograph entries shown as separate, equally-weighted cards** (2026-07-05, built) — a real JMdict-sourced dated/archaic/obsolete tag where the entry's own `m`/`ki` data carries one, visual demotion otherwise. Validated against 酒/君 and 私/あたし; the tag check was later widened to every entry, not just duplicates (2026-07-06). See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **お's interjection-sense ranking** (2026-07-05) — accepted as a permanent limitation, same grounds as 入る/まま: no TUBELEX data exists for お at all, so there's nothing to fix without weakening an unrelated, still-valid tiebreak. See Decisions Log.
- **輝ける (godan potential-form) and 来れる (ら抜き of 来る) returning no entry or the wrong sense** (2026-07-05) — fixed by a reverse-conjugation lookup fallback preferring an irregular match over a coincidental godan one; 8/8 correct on the 3-show corpus. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **おぉ returning no dictionary entry** (2026-07-05) — fixed via a small-vowel-elongation lookup fallback (lookup-layer only, doesn't touch displayed text). See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **ふ showing wrong/unrelated senses for its interjection use** (2026-07-05) — confirmed a genuine upstream JMdict gap (no `int`-tagged entry for ふ in the raw release at all), not a local bug. Nothing to fix; accepted as a data-source limitation.
- **チヤホヤ (mid-word katakana proper-noun mis-suppression)** (2026-07-05) — cause was groupTokens' katakana-proper-noun rule silencing a whole run on one mis-tagged token, not the ellipsis first suspected; fixed by an un-suppression pass. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **入る (いる/はいる ordering) and まま (まんま/にゅうぼ ordering)** (2026-07-05) — both accepted as permanent limitations, same grounds as the kanji-heteronym precedent: no frequency data exists to break either tie without hardcoding. See Decisions Log.
- **じゃない showing no inflection note** (2026-07-06) — confirmed correct existing behavior, not a bug: it resolves via the phrase-fuse mechanism (own JMdict headword), and inflection notes are only ever generated for grouped constructions, not fused phrase spans — same as んだ/からといって. See Decisions Log.
- **Archaic/dated-tagged entries interacting with the frequency-rank badge** (2026-07-06) — no change needed: the existing "no badge without TUBELEX data" rule already gives the right result, since such terms essentially never carry usage data. See Decisions Log.
- **Karaoke-style word highlighting** (2026-07-06) — considered and declined, not deferred: no per-word timing data exists anywhere in the pipeline, and an approximation would drift out of sync almost immediately. Same shape of rejection as the mono-ruby naive-heuristic call. See Decisions Log.
- **Kanji spellings on homograph cards, numbered gloss display + gloss-cap bump** (2026-07-06) — both built via one combined `jmdict-compact.json` regeneration, validated at 100% match on all non-gloss fields across 462,753 index keys. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **それもそうか-style multi-word idiomatic dual-view** (2026-07-06) — no new code needed: それもそうか has no JMdict headword, but its close variants do and all already fuse correctly through the general phrase-matcher. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **Manual subtitle upload fallback (.srt/.ass)** (2026-07-06) — built: `subtitle-parser.js` added as a content script, file-picker UI feeds it directly via `FileReader`, no background.js round-trip. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **Numbered-gloss structure, kanji-spelling display placement, and だろ's missing inflection note** (2026-07-06, second Frieren round) — all confirmed working as designed, no fixes made. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **First Witch Hat Atelier live-testing round** (2026-07-12) — six confirmed bugs fixed and two fact-finding items resolved correct-as-is. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **`jmdict-compact.json`'s 3-sense cap losing meanings that matter** (2026-07-13) — confirmed real, resolved by raising the cap to 8 for particle/interjection-primary entries only, after a full raw-release scan rather than raising it globally. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **Second Witch Hat Atelier live-testing round** (2026-07-13) — an ordering regression traced to a missed regen-pipeline step (now documented as required), plus three further bugs fixed and four items confirmed correct as-is. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **よ popup showing a bare "hey; you" with no usage context** (2026-07-15) — fixed by adding JMdict's `s_inf` sense annotations to the compact format and popup, scoped universally rather than to particle/interjection entries. See Section 5 and Decisions Log.
- **Show/episode auto-detection from the Crunchyroll page** (2026-07-15) — built on Crunchyroll's schema.org JSON-LD after page inspection found the planned TMDB/AniList ID route assumed data that isn't there; plain title matching verified sufficient. See Section 5 (Phase 4.5) and Decisions Log.
- **Does Crunchyroll's episode-to-episode navigation reload the page?** (2026-07-15) — no, it's in-page SPA navigation. Detected by polling `location.pathname`, after a `history.pushState` wrapper failed: an isolated-world content script can't intercept main-world history calls. See Section 5 (Phase 4.5) and Decisions Log.
- **Frequency-rank badge exact numeric thresholds** (2026-07-19) — locked in at absolute TUBELEX counts (rare < 20 <= uncommon < 500 <= common), chosen from real distribution data and boundary-word spot-checks. See Decisions Log.
- **Whether the TUBELEX coverage gap hits content words as well as function words** (2026-07-19) — both, with content words the larger share (7,737 entries in the final id-based build). See Decisions Log.
- **Phase 4's live re-test** (2026-07-15) — formally closed by explicit user decision (not exhaustive completion) after three shows and several testing rounds; more issues may surface later and will be fixed as found. See Section 6 (Phase 4) and Decisions Log.
- **Does Jimaku's API expose any per-file popularity/usage signal?** (2026-07-15) — no, confirmed against real responses at both file and entry level, so "best subtitle" ranking has to fall back to the hardcoded uploader-preference list. See Decisions Log.
- **Which specific groups go on the hardcoded uploader-preference list** (2026-07-15) — seeded from uploaders already seen working in this project's own live-testing (SubsPlease, Haruhana, VCB-Studio), to be refined later. See Section 5 (Phase 4.5).

---

## How to use this doc

**project-plan.md is the full source of truth.** `CLAUDE.md` is a short session-start briefing (goals, litmus test, current priorities, working-style rules) that points back here for detail — it should not duplicate this document's content, only summarize what's needed to start a session.

### Formatting rules — read before editing this document

These are not suggestions; a session that writes into this doc without following them needs to be corrected before the next session starts. If you (Claude Code) find yourself about to write a paragraph into Section 6, or a decision title that's a full sentence, stop and re-read this list.

- **Decisions Log entries are ALWAYS `**Date — Short bolded title.** Reasoning/rejected-alternative in plain text after.`** The bolded part is a short label (under ~12 words) someone could scan in a list of 50 entries and immediately know what it's about — never a full recap of what happened. Bad: `**2026-07-03 — After investigating whether kuromoji's reading field could be used to disambiguate 一行 in context, we determined it could not because of a segmentation quirk and inconsistent behavior on other heteronyms, so we logged it as an accepted limitation.**` Good: `**2026-07-03 — Kanji-heteronym sense-context disambiguation (一行) rejected as an anchor-to-kuromoji-reading fix.**` followed by the detail as normal sentences below.
- **Only genuine decisions go in the Decisions Log.** Test: "what did you choose this over?" If the honest answer is "there was nothing else to do" — confirming an API contract, discovering a platform limitation, verifying a fact — it's a finding, and it belongs in Section 5 (Technical Architecture) instead, dated inline, not logged as a decision.
- **If a decision revises an earlier one, don't add a new entry — find the original and update it** (or add a one-line "(Superseded on [date] — see below)" pointer if the revision is big enough to deserve its own entry, as with the こと sense-ordering deferral). Do not let the same decision appear twice with two different titles.
- **Section 5 (Technical Architecture) is organized by category, not a flat list.** General/cross-cutting architecture first, then grouped by build phase in Section 6's own order (Phase 1–2, Phase 3, Phase 4, Phase 4.5, Phase 5, Phase 6–7). When adding a new entry, place it under the phase it actually belongs to (usually the phase where it was built or where the bug was found) — don't just append to the end of the section. If a phase category doesn't exist yet because nothing's been built for it, add a one-line placeholder (see Phase 6–7) rather than omitting the heading.
- **Section 5 (Technical Architecture) entries are current-state descriptions, not session logs.** Each bullet describes what's true *now*, with the date it became true in parentheses inline. If something was fixed, then fixed again differently, only describe the final state — don't keep the superseded description around "for history." History lives in the Decisions Log.
- **Section 6 (Build Order) "Done" bullets are one line each**, summarizing what shipped, not narrating how it was built or why. If a bug fix needs more than one line to explain, that explanation belongs in Section 5 (what changed technically) and/or Section 7 (what was decided) — the Build Order bullet just says what got fixed and points there implicitly (it doesn't even need "see Section 5" on every line; that's the default assumption for this doc).
- **Section 8 (Open Questions) entries are 2–4 sentences max.** State the question, why it's open, and what would resolve it. Do not paste a full investigation narrative — if the investigation is worth preserving, it's a Decisions Log entry (if concluded) or belongs summarized in Technical Architecture (if it's a standing limitation).
- **Ongoing questions are split into "Needs testing or real-world feedback" and "Needs an actual decision."** Test for which bucket: would a test/corpus result/usage data resolve this on its own, or does a person need to weigh options and choose? If neither applies — the reasoning is already fully worked out and accepted — it's not Ongoing at all; it belongs in Resolved (or wasn't a genuine open question in the first place).
- **Before adding anything to Resolved, check it isn't already fully covered by a Decisions Log entry or a Section 5 entry.** A one-line pointer ("see Decisions Log [date]") is the right shape; restating that entry's reasoning in full is duplication and should be trimmed to just the pointer, or dropped from Section 8 entirely if the pointer is all there'd be.
- **Never duplicate the same fact across two sections.** If a technical fact and the decision behind it are both worth recording, split them: the *current state* goes in Section 5, the *reasoning/rejected alternative* goes in Section 7, and each references the other only if needed — don't restate one inside the other in full.
- **Never cross-reference an Open Questions or Build Order item by number** (e.g. "see Ongoing #3"). These lists get reordered whenever an item resolves and the rest renumber — a numbered reference goes stale silently the very next edit. Reference by name instead (e.g. "see Ongoing, 'Forced-subtitle overlay behavior'"), which stays correct regardless of position.
- **Update the `_Last updated:_` date at the top of the file whenever you edit it.**

### End-of-session update checklist

1. Update Section 6 (Build Order): move completed items from "remaining" to "done" **as one-line bullets** (see formatting rules above), add any newly-discovered remaining items in priority order.
2. Scan the session for genuine decisions (per the test above). Add qualifying ones to Section 7 with a short bolded title, in date order. If a decision revises an earlier one, consolidate — don't duplicate.
3. Update Section 8: move resolved questions to "Resolved" (one line, pointing to the Decisions Log entry that resolved it if there is one — don't restate its reasoning); add new ones to "Ongoing" under the correct subsection (needs testing/feedback vs. needs a decision), in phase-priority order within each, in 2–4 sentences each.
4. If a real architectural or technical detail changed, update Section 5 directly — describe the new current state, don't append a second description alongside the old one.
5. Check Section 3 (Competitive Landscape) and Section 4 (V1 Scope) for drift — e.g. a feature moving between "differentiation" and "core loop," or new competitor findings — and update in place rather than leaving stale claims.
6. Update the `_Last updated:_` date at the top of this document.
7. If nothing decision-worthy happened (pure implementation, no surprises), say so explicitly in the session's chat response rather than skipping the check silently — but this doc itself doesn't need an edit in that case.
